**[00:44:09.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000105)  
Today, the Committee on Finance will deliberate the proposed budget of the DOF, its attached agencies, and cooperation for the fiscal year 2027,

**[00:44:19.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000106)  
and discuss relevant points in strengthening our economy.

**[00:44:22.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000109)  
Our revenue efforts, managing debt levels, and expanding our financial resources.

**[00:44:28.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000110)  
The Department of Finance plays a critical role in ensuring that the government has sufficient resources to fund our priorities,

**[00:44:36.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000111)  
from healthcare and education to infrastructure, agriculture, social protection, and other essential services.

**[00:44:45.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000112)  
To fund government programs and projects, revenue collection is critical.

**[00:44:49.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000114)  
Kailangan nating malaman ang improvement sa

**[00:44:52.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000115)  
BIR tax administration, digitalization at enforcement ay talagang nagresulta sa mas mataas na koleksyon,

**[00:45:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000116)  
mas maayos na compliance at mas kaunting tax leakages.

**[00:45:04.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000117)  
On the other hand, Bureau of Customs continues to face persistent challenges

**[00:45:09.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000118)  
in combating smuggling and illicit trade.

**[00:45:12.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000119)  
Kaya nais nating malaman sa kabila ng mas mababang budget ngayong taon,

**[00:45:17.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000120)  
kaya ba nating palakasin ang border protection,

**[00:45:20.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000121)  
masugpo ang smuggling at mapataas ang revenue collection.

**[00:45:23.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000122)  
As the government continues to operate within a challenging fiscal environment

**[00:45:30.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000123)  
and as we seek additional revenues, equally important are sound debt management,

**[00:45:36.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000124)  
responsible borrowing, and prudent spending.

**[00:45:39.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000125)  
So in discussing the proposed budget today, we want clear answers on three basic questions.

**[00:45:46.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000126)  
Tama at sapat ba ang ating kinukulekta?

**[00:45:49.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000127)  
Maayos at efficient ba nating ginagamit ang bawat piso?

**[00:45:52.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000128)  
At responsable ba nating pinamamahalan ang ating utang at fiscal obligations

**[00:45:58.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000129)  
upang hindi nating ipasa sa susunod na henerasyon?

**[00:46:02.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000130)  
As our economy and fiscal needs continue to evolve,

**[00:46:05.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000131)  
we must ensure that our country remains in a stable and sustainable fiscal position.

**[00:46:11.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000132)  
And we owe it to the Filipino taxpayers to ensure that every peso requested

**[00:46:15.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000133)  
is a peso efficiently and responsibly spent.

**[00:46:19.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000134)  
Today's hearing gives us an opportunity

**[00:46:20.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000135)  
to examine whether we can achieve a sustainable future

**[00:46:22.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000136)  
and whether our revenue strategies, borrowings, investments,

**[00:46:27.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000137)  
and financing mechanisms are putting us on the right track,

**[00:46:31.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000138)  
not only for the coming year but for the years ahead.

**[00:46:34.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000139)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:47:43.520] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000161)  
Thank you, Chairperson Senator JV Ejercito,

**[00:47:47.520] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000162)  
Vice Chairperson, Senator Kiko Pangilinan,

**[00:47:51.280] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000163)  
Senate Committee Member,

**[00:47:52.640] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000164)  
members, colleagues in government, good afternoon.

**[00:47:55.060] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000165)  
Thank you for this opportunity to present the proposed

**[00:47:57.960] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000166)  
fiscal year 2027 budget of the Department of Finance,

**[00:48:02.760] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000167)  
its bureaus, and attached agencies.

**[00:48:06.020] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000168)  
The DOF has a critical mandate,

**[00:48:08.420] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000169)  
raise resources the government needs,

**[00:48:11.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000170)  
manage them responsibly,

**[00:48:13.060] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000171)  
and protect the country's fiscal stability.

**[00:48:16.000] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000172)  
We are the government's fundraiser,

**[00:48:18.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000173)  
but raising revenue is only one part of the job.

**[00:48:22.640] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000174)  
We also have to manage debt,

**[00:48:24.820] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000175)  
strengthen financial systems,

**[00:48:27.300] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000176)  
improve tax and customs administration,

**[00:48:29.840] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000177)  
and make sure that the government has the fiscal space

**[00:48:32.540] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000178)  
to fund its priorities.

**[00:48:36.260] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000179)  
When this administration took office in 2022,

**[00:48:39.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000180)  
the country was carrying 12.79 trillion

**[00:48:42.200] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000181)  
in national government debt,

**[00:48:45.200] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000182)  
much of it accumulated during the pandemic.

**[00:48:48.200] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000183)  
Our task since then has been clear,

**[00:48:51.320] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000184)  
rebuild fiscal space,

**[00:48:52.640] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000185)  
while continuing to support growth

**[00:48:54.520] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000186)  
and invest in the country's future.

**[00:48:57.520] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000187)  
As a response,

**[00:48:58.500] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000188)  
we crafted the country's medium-term fiscal framework in 2022,

**[00:49:02.920] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000189)  
the government's blueprint for reducing our deficit and debt

**[00:49:05.900] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000190)  
as a percentage of GDP,

**[00:49:08.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000191)  
while creating more jobs,

**[00:49:10.020] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000192)  
increasing people's incomes,

**[00:49:11.940] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000193)  
and reducing poverty.

**[00:49:14.200] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000194)  
It set us on a path of gradual and sustained fiscal consolidation

**[00:49:18.560] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000195)  
to restore fiscal space,

**[00:49:20.860] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000196)  
while continuing to invest in the future,

**[00:49:22.620] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000197)  
while continuing to invest in the foundations of growth.

**[00:49:25.100] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000198)  
But our recovery has not happened in an easy environment.

**[00:49:29.840] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000199)  
Over the years,

**[00:49:30.580] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000200)  
external pressures and global uncertainties

**[00:49:32.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000201)  
have disrupted supply chains

**[00:49:34.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000202)  
and pushed inflation up,

**[00:49:36.740] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000203)  
affecting the pace of growth.

**[00:49:39.120] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000204)  
In this context,

**[00:49:40.440] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000205)  
we needed to recalibrate our growth and fiscal targets

**[00:49:43.300] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000206)  
to keep them realistic and responsive

**[00:49:45.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000207)  
to evolving economic conditions.

**[00:49:47.980] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000208)  
But the direction of our fiscal policy

**[00:49:50.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000209)  
has remained consistent.

**[00:49:51.900] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000210)  
We have been working hard to ensure that

**[00:49:52.600] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000211)  
we have pursued stronger revenue collection,

**[00:49:55.240] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000212)  
disciplined expenditure,

**[00:49:57.020] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000213)  
and prudent debt management.

**[00:50:00.280] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000214)  
Total revenues have grown by an average of 10.54% annually

**[00:50:05.360] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000215)  
over the last four years.

**[00:50:09.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000216)  
And in 2025,

**[00:50:11.040] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000217)  
the BIR surpassed 3 trillion pesos

**[00:50:13.940] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000218)  
in tax collections for the first time.

**[00:50:17.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000219)  
We continue to strengthen collections

**[00:50:19.240] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000220)  
while mobilizing additional non-tax revenues

**[00:50:21.800] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000221)  
and managing the economy.

**[00:50:22.600] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000222)  
As a result,

**[00:50:26.820] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000223)  
the fiscal deficit has continued to narrow

**[00:50:29.120] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000224)  
as we remain on track

**[00:50:31.180] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000225)  
toward our medium-term consolidation targets.

**[00:50:35.100] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000226)  
From 8.6% in 2021

**[00:50:37.300] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000227)  
and 7.3% in 2022,

**[00:50:40.240] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000228)  
we are bringing it down to 5.4%

**[00:50:43.100] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000229)  
by the end of 2026.

**[00:50:47.280] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000230)  
Our debt indicators also remain manageable.

**[00:50:50.440] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000231)  
In 2025,

**[00:50:51.700] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000232)  
national government debt stood at 63.2% of GDP

**[00:50:55.700] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000233)  
while general government debt settled at 56.8% of GDP,

**[00:51:01.060] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000234)  
well below the 70% international benchmark

**[00:51:04.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000235)  
for debt sustainability

**[00:51:05.640] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000236)  
and within a manageable range

**[00:51:08.260] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000237)  
compared with other emerging economies.

**[00:51:11.680] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000238)  
More importantly,

**[00:51:13.440] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000239)  
we have maintained a prudent debt mix,

**[00:51:16.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000240)  
predominantly domestic,

**[00:51:18.280] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000241)  
predominantly carrying fixed interest rates,

**[00:51:20.300] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000242)  
and predominantly,

**[00:51:21.700] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000243)  
structured with long repayment terms

**[00:51:24.480] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000244)  
of at least five years.

**[00:51:28.540] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000245)  
First,

**[00:51:29.500] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000246)  
67% of our debt is domestic.

**[00:51:33.700] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000247)  
This reduces our exposure to foreign exchange risks

**[00:51:36.620] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000248)  
while also supporting the continued development

**[00:51:39.400] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000249)  
of our local capital market.

**[00:51:41.980] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000250)  
Second,

**[00:51:42.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000251)  
90% of our debt carries fixed interest rates.

**[00:51:46.020] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000252)  
This provides greater certainty

**[00:51:47.520] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000253)  
in our debt servicing costs

**[00:51:48.980] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000254)  
and shields us from sudden increases

**[00:51:51.140] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000255)  
or fluctuations

**[00:51:51.700] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000256)  
in global interest rates.

**[00:51:54.380] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000257)  
And third,

**[00:51:55.580] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000258)  
84.1% of our debt

**[00:51:57.860] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000259)  
has long-term repayment periods,

**[00:52:00.560] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000260)  
meaning we have several years

**[00:52:01.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000261)  
before these obligations fall due.

**[00:52:05.440] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000262)  
These generally refer to debt

**[00:52:07.880] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000263)  
with maturities of over five years.

**[00:52:11.520] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000264)  
This longer maturity profile

**[00:52:13.740] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000265)  
reduces our refinancing risks

**[00:52:15.720] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000266)  
and gives us greater predictability

**[00:52:17.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000267)  
in managing our debt obligations.

**[00:52:20.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000268)  
This strategic,

**[00:52:21.700] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000269)  
borrowing mix

**[00:52:22.640] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000270)  
strengthened our resilience

**[00:52:24.240] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000271)  
to external shocks

**[00:52:25.260] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000272)  
and made our debt obligations

**[00:52:27.120] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000273)  
more predictable

**[00:52:27.840] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000274)

**[01:04:29.320] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000642)

**[01:12:56.360] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000880)

**[01:12:57.820] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000881)

**[01:12:58.520] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000882)

**[01:13:01.120] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000883)  
Your Honor, with regard to the estimates,

**[01:13:05.220] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000884)

**[01:13:10.060] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000885)

**[01:13:11.920] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000886)  
is that we're losing a minimum

**[01:13:13.960] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000887)

**[01:13:16.900] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000888)  
When I asked,

**[01:13:19.180] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000889)  
why is this?

**[01:13:20.520] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000890)  
Different from the 100 billion plus

**[01:13:22.280] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000891)  
that I heard over the news,

**[01:13:24.200] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000892)  
the explanations, of course,

**[01:13:25.680] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000893)

**[01:13:28.780] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000894)  
to the existence of,

**[01:13:31.020] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000895)

**[01:13:32.660] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000896)  
wherein the data coming from

**[01:13:36.080] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000897)

**[01:13:38.980] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000898)  
did not account for the imports

**[01:13:43.520] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000899)  
that would go directly to the export zones,

**[01:13:46.500] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000900)  
which are supposed to be not intended to...

**[01:13:50.520] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000901)  
The raw materials being used

**[01:13:51.980] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000902)

**[01:13:54.420] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000903)

**[01:13:55.940] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000904)  
But still,

**[01:13:57.060] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000905)  
even with the estimate of 30 billion,

**[01:13:59.200] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000906)

**[01:14:00.860] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000907)  
even if it's the most conservative

**[01:14:02.240] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000908)

**[01:14:05.560] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000909)  
But with regard to the second question...

**[01:14:09.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000910)  
Just to clarify, Commissioner,

**[01:14:11.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000911)

**[01:14:12.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000912)  
What is your estimate

**[01:14:13.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000913)  
of the number of containers

**[01:14:15.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000914)

**[01:14:17.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000915)

**[01:14:20.520] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000916)  
2 million a year,

**[01:14:23.440] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000917)  
so divided by 360.

**[01:14:25.560] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000918)  
That's 80,000, right?

**[01:14:27.420] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000919)  
Roughly 80,000.

**[01:14:29.000] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000920)  
We call it, Mr. Senator, TEUs.

**[01:14:31.960] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000921)  
TEUs would represent

**[01:14:33.200] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000922)  
yung debente or 20-footer container.

**[01:14:35.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000923)  
Yes.

**[01:14:36.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000924)  
So if that's 2 million a year,

**[01:14:39.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000925)  
a day.

**[01:14:43.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000926)  
About 5,500 a day.

**[01:14:45.960] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000927)  
A day.

**[01:14:46.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000928)  
And clearly,

**[01:14:50.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000929)  
if you don't have that single window,

**[01:14:53.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000930)

**[01:14:55.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000931)  
then we cannot really monitor

**[01:14:56.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000932)

**[01:14:59.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000933)  
Thank you.

**[01:15:00.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000934)  
Di ba? Because all of that will now be, how do you call it, inputted into the national single window for better monitoring, entry, etc. Di ba? I mean, that's my understanding.

**[01:15:15.520] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000935)  
With regard to the number of containers, nakukuha naman po natin yan dahil these are being reported fairly accurately by the port operators.

**[01:15:30.580] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000936)  
But what we cannot accurately measure will be the real values supposed to be contained in those importations.

**[01:15:40.240] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000937)  
So the NSW, of course, would be able to help a lot.

**[01:15:43.820] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000938)  
Insofar as getting the accurate values from these importations.

**[01:15:48.120] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000939)  
But if I may, Your Honor, recognizing the complexity of the NSW project, what BOC has done when we came in July last year is to embark on our own, of course, with the support of the Secretary of Finance, Sec. Frederick, we are now embarking on our own customs processing system.

**[01:16:10.580] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000940)  
Paliwanag ko lang po ng konti briefly.

**[01:16:13.820] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000941)  
CPS will remove the human intervention involved in the processes of the importations.

**[01:16:22.940] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000942)  
Ganito po yan. We have identified 15 ICT projects. At the center of this is the CPS, Customs Processing System.

**[01:16:30.540] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000943)  
Ganito po yan. CPS, if compared to Singapore CPS, they call it NSW.

**[01:16:37.540] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000944)  
Mabilis lang silang gumawa ng NSW na, which they were able to easily compel.

**[01:16:43.820] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000945)  
We have identified 15 other agencies dealing with the Singapore Customs to cooperate with the Singapore Customs NSW.

**[01:16:51.860] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000946)  
Now that we have our own SW, which is not being led by BOC, what we did is to embark with our own CPS, which will be similar to the NSW.

**[01:17:04.260] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000947)  
Ganito po yan. So lahat ng merong...

**[01:17:07.340] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000948)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:17:09.160] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000949)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:17:09.580] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000950)  
But we have to prioritize and we should not be...

**[01:17:13.820] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000951)  
dependent on the NSW per se because that's not within our full control.

**[01:17:18.820] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000952)  
What our CPS will do once we roll this out middle of next year is to prioritize, for example, agencies such as the Department of Agriculture,

**[01:17:28.340] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000953)  
the, well, FDA sana, but FDA also has some issues, PNP, DTI, yung mga mabilis na ho nating isama.

**[01:17:40.260] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000954)  
So the data will be automatic real time.

**[01:17:43.820] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000955)  
From the...

**[01:17:44.840] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000956)  
With the complete loop from the submission of the import documents or, of course, export documents, assessment, payment after releasing, including, Your Honor, the bank documents.

**[01:17:56.320] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000957)  
We have to include the bank documents because, of course, we assume that the banks will never collude with the smugglers because they have a bigger name and a stake to protect.

**[01:18:06.360] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000958)  
So with our own CPS, we will be able to approximate what a full NSW can do.

**[01:18:13.820] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000959)  
But the difference is we will just be prioritizing the agencies which are crucial to our operations. Of course, ideally, more than 40 plus agencies should be able to integrate real-time in terms of providing us the permits and all the relevant documents that will be vital in the computations of the values that we will be able to base our collections from and to be able to say yes or no, which importations which will be allowed or not.

**[01:18:43.820] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000960)  
So, yun naman po ang good news. Now, with the question, how are we funding this? Wala po ang choice dahil wala po sa budget yan ng 2026. So, we have started with the PPP proposed to us. Nasa legal and technical discussions na po kami. And we're confident that this can be rolled out with the pilot areas middle of next year, meaning June, July, Your Honor.

**[01:19:11.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000961)  
And what would the pilot areas be? Do you already, have you identified that? Or it's still?

**[01:19:18.500] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000962)  
Yes, sir. But initial lang po yan. We might first try with the Port of Batangas. It's a huge port but not the biggest port. At medyo masimple po yung pasok doon ng mga containers. And then one airport. And then another port outside Metro Manila.

**[01:19:35.200] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000963)  
Because we have to eventually integrate 17 major ports and 39 sub-ports. We have a total of 56.

**[01:19:41.900] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-000964)  
ports to be included in the program.

**[01:19:46.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-000965)  
Okay, Usec Ligon, will you also update us with the National Single Window?

**[01:19:52.820] DOF Undersecretary Rolando T. Ligon Jr.** (asr-000966)

**[01:20:03.900] DOF Undersecretary Rolando T. Ligon Jr.** (asr-000967)

**[01:20:11.900] DOF Undersecretary Rolando T. Ligon Jr.** (asr-000968)

**[01:21:41.140] DOF Undersecretary Rolando T. Ligon Jr.** (asr-000983)  
In one year and a half.

**[01:21:41.900] DOF Undersecretary Rolando T. Ligon Jr.** (asr-000984)

**[01:22:06.780] DOF Undersecretary Rolando T. Ligon Jr.** (asr-000985)

**[01:22:07.500] DOF Undersecretary Rolando T. Ligon Jr.** (asr-000986)

**[01:22:16.720] DOF Undersecretary Rolando T. Ligon Jr.** (asr-000988)

**[01:22:17.200] DOF Undersecretary Rolando T. Ligon Jr.** (asr-000989)

So...

It makes me...

**[01:28:12.480] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001076)

**[01:28:15.260] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001077)

**[01:28:16.020] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001078)

**[01:28:20.360] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001079)

**[01:28:26.720] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001080)

**[01:28:35.680] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001081)

**[01:28:39.840] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001082)

**[01:28:43.360] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001083)

**[01:28:50.520] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001085)

**[01:28:54.480] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001086)

**[01:28:58.180] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001087)

**[01:29:03.080] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001088)

**[01:29:07.100] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001089)

**[01:29:09.820] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001093)

**[01:29:12.740] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001094)

**[01:29:16.700] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001095)

**[01:29:17.160] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001096)

**[01:29:20.200] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001097)

**[01:29:30.180] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-001100)

**[01:29:44.500] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001101-split-2)

asking for additional budget of P10 billion for your consideration from the BOC.

**[01:30:07.040] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001103)

**[01:30:12.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001104)  
And then the BIR also has a request of about a little over a billion pesos

**[01:30:17.220] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001105)  
of necessary budget that they need for their operations.

**[01:30:21.720] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001106)

**[01:30:24.120] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001107)  
Actually, the amount cut down obviously was much larger.

**[01:30:26.400] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001108)  
But in the prioritization, they have a wish list of about a billion pesos.

**[01:30:31.320] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001109)

**[01:34:50.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001176)

**[01:34:55.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001177)

I don't know,

in terms of credit

**[01:35:45.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001195)  
I don't know.

**[01:35:46.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001196)

**[01:35:48.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001197)  
So,

**[01:35:49.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001198)  
so,

**[01:35:50.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001199)  
you don't have to answer it now,

but,

For example,

**[01:35:59.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001203)

therefore,

that if they have the market,

I mean,

among others.

And therefore,

So,

**[01:36:51.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001224)  
so,

**[01:36:51.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001225)  
so,

**[01:37:26.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001247)

**[01:37:26.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001248)  
we're doing benchmarking.

**[01:37:28.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001249)  
The reason why we're doing this now

**[01:37:30.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001250)  
and gathering the information

**[01:37:31.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001251)

**[01:37:32.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001252)  
we anticipate that,

**[01:37:33.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001253)  
and the President has identified it

**[01:37:36.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001254)

**[01:37:38.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001255)  
as a priority,

**[01:37:40.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001256)  
two priority measures.

**[01:37:41.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001257)  
The renationalization

**[01:37:44.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001258)

**[01:37:45.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001259)

**[01:37:47.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001260)  
which means

**[01:37:48.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001261)

And the reestablishment

**[01:37:51.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001263)

**[01:37:54.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001264)  
cooperatives

**[01:37:56.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001267)  
under the DA.

**[01:37:58.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001268)  
So we want to benchmark that

**[01:38:00.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001269)  
because once that is low,

**[01:38:02.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001270)  
then we will now create

**[01:38:04.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001271)  
the information necessary

**[01:38:06.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001272)  
for us to have benchmarked

**[01:38:09.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001273)  
in terms of where are we now.

**[01:38:11.580] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001274)  
Because once you have

**[01:38:12.940] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001275)  
extension workers

**[01:38:14.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001276)  
in every municipality,

**[01:38:17.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001277)  
right now, for example,

**[01:38:18.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001278)  
in San Luis, Pampanga,

**[01:38:20.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001279)  
they have 3,500 farmers,

**[01:38:23.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001280)  
but they only have

**[01:38:24.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001281)

**[01:38:29.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001282)  
Unlike in Taiwan,

**[01:38:31.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001283)  
the ratio of extension workers

**[01:38:33.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001284)  
to farmers

**[01:38:34.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001285)  
is one extension worker,

**[01:38:36.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001286)  
18 farmers.

**[01:38:39.080] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001287)  
They have 580,000 farmers.

**[01:38:42.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001288)  
They're organized

**[01:38:43.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001289)  
into 320 farmers associations.

**[01:38:47.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001290)  
And of these 320 farmers associations,

**[01:38:50.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001291)  
their members are co-ops.

**[01:38:52.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001292)  
So the co-ops become members

**[01:38:54.420] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001293)  
of the association

**[01:38:55.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001294)  
and they're organized

**[01:38:57.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001295)  
and have extension worker support

**[01:39:00.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001296)  
of 18 farmers,

**[01:39:03.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001297)  
one extension worker.

**[01:39:05.220] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001298)  
So you can imagine

**[01:39:06.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001299)  
if we restructure the DA,

**[01:39:09.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001300)

**[01:39:11.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001301)  
for extension workers,

**[01:39:13.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001302)  
financial literacy programs,

**[01:39:15.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001303)  
interventions,

**[01:39:16.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001304)  
capacity building, training,

**[01:39:18.020] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001305)  
and then create the cooperatives,

**[01:39:20.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001306)

**[01:39:21.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-001307)  
And then you will be able to

I mean, for lack of a better term.

So that's why we want that data.

**[01:41:21.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001376)  
Rising national debt,

**[01:41:23.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001377)  
as of July 2026,

**[01:41:24.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001378)

**[01:41:26.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001379)  
climbed to 19.39 trillion,

**[01:41:30.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001380)  
with debt to GDP

**[01:41:32.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001381)  
sitting at 66%,

**[01:41:34.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001382)  
and is projected to breach

**[01:41:35.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001383)  
21.48 trillion

**[01:41:37.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001384)  
by the end of 2027.

**[01:41:40.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001385)  
To fund the upcoming fiscal year,

**[01:41:42.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001386)  
the DOF expects to borrow

**[01:41:44.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001387)  
an additional 3.3 trillion.

**[01:41:48.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001388)  
This is very concerning

**[01:41:49.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001389)  
as debt servicing alone

**[01:41:51.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001390)  
under financial expenses,

**[01:41:53.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001391)  
will be at 1.114 trillion

**[01:41:56.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001392)  
of the national debt budget.

**[01:41:58.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001393)  
That's over a trillion pesos

**[01:42:00.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001394)  
going to interest

**[01:42:01.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001395)  
rather than building hospitals,

**[01:42:03.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001396)

**[01:42:04.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001397)

**[01:42:08.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001398)  
My question is,

**[01:42:09.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001399)  
what concrete revenue measures

**[01:42:10.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001400)  
or strategies is the DOF implementing

**[01:42:12.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001401)  
to ensure that these massive interest payments

**[01:42:16.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001402)  
don't crowd out our spending

**[01:42:17.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001403)

First,

**[01:42:44.600] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001414)  
what we look at,

**[01:42:46.800] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001415)

**[01:42:48.300] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001416)

**[01:42:49.480] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001417)

**[01:42:53.220] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001418)  
and GDP.

**[01:42:54.440] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001419)  
In short,

**[01:42:54.880] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001420)  
GG debt

**[01:42:55.560] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001421)

**[01:42:57.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001422)

**[01:42:59.420] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001423)  
So yes,

**[01:42:59.920] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001424)  
NG debt is roughly around 66%.

**[01:43:02.320] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001425)

**[01:43:04.740] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001426)  
And the World Bank threshold

**[01:43:06.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001427)  
right now for a country

**[01:43:09.520] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001428)

**[01:43:12.320] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001429)

**[01:43:15.260] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001430)

**[01:43:16.480] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001431)

**[01:43:19.120] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001432)

**[01:43:21.000] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001433)

**[01:43:22.840] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001434)

**[01:43:24.560] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001435)

**[01:43:26.160] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001436)

**[01:43:30.340] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001437)

**[01:43:31.560] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001438)

**[01:43:37.740] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001439)  
Those are my two replies.

**[01:43:40.500] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001440)  
But I think the most critical reply

**[01:43:42.760] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001441)

**[01:43:55.820] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001446)  
all the other,

I'm the only cabinet secretary

**[01:44:00.960] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001449)

So I think the equation

**[01:44:11.440] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001457)  
of the gap

**[01:46:46.020] Treasurer of the Philippines Sharon P. Almanza** (asr-001541)

**[01:46:47.540] Treasurer of the Philippines Sharon P. Almanza** (asr-001542)

**[01:46:49.960] Treasurer of the Philippines Sharon P. Almanza** (asr-001543)

**[01:46:59.400] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001549)  
Mr. Chair, if I may,

**[01:47:08.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001553)

**[01:47:10.580] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001554)

**[01:47:12.500] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001555)

**[01:47:13.740] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001556)  
and manageable.

**[01:47:16.360] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001557)  
There are three ways, right? One is we want to

**[01:47:18.480] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001558)

**[01:47:20.560] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001559)

**[01:47:22.920] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001560)  
So right now it's about 6733.

**[01:47:25.540] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001561)  
Our goal is always to

**[01:47:26.740] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001562)

**[01:47:28.760] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001563)

**[01:47:30.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001564)

**[01:47:32.760] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001565)  
foreign currency.

**[01:47:34.500] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001566)  
Second, we try to borrow always

**[01:47:36.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001567)

**[01:47:38.580] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001568)  
meaning over five years.

**[01:47:42.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001569)  
But I think

**[01:47:42.640] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001570)  
what Treasurer Almanza was

**[01:47:44.540] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001571)

**[01:47:46.520] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001572)

**[01:47:48.700] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001573)

**[01:47:50.420] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001574)  
we may have to shift to shorter

**[01:47:52.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001575)  
term borrowings because it's cheaper.

**[01:47:55.640] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001576)  
And third is

**[01:47:56.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001577)  
the fixed rates. So we

**[01:47:58.260] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001578)  
whenever we borrow, we try to borrow

**[01:48:00.300] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001579)

**[01:48:02.420] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001580)  
90% of our debt is

**[01:48:04.440] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001581)

**[01:48:06.280] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001582)  
interest rates. So at least there's

**[01:48:08.400] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001583)  
predictability in the

**[01:48:10.420] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001584)  
interest payments of the country.

**[01:48:13.300] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001585)  
Thank you, Mr.

**[01:48:14.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001586)  
Chair.

**[02:15:47.580] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002364)

**[02:15:48.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002365)

**[02:15:52.600] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002367)

**[02:15:54.840] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002368)

**[02:15:57.860] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002369)  
And the Board

**[02:15:58.880] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002370)  
of Investments should

**[02:16:00.400] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002371)

**[02:16:02.740] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002372)

**[02:16:06.680] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002374)

**[02:16:08.800] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002375)

**[02:16:13.060] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002377)

**[02:16:14.820] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002378)

**[02:16:16.880] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002379)

**[02:16:19.760] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002380)

**[02:16:21.040] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002381)

**[02:16:22.360] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002382)

**[02:16:25.180] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002383)  
to avoid VAT

**[02:16:27.040] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002384)

**[02:16:28.680] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002385)

**[02:16:30.640] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002386)

**[02:16:32.820] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002387)

**[02:16:35.420] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002388)

**[02:16:36.920] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002389)  
the idea there is you're generally

**[02:16:39.300] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002390)

**[02:16:41.120] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002391)

**[02:16:43.620] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002392)

**[02:16:45.800] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002393)

**[02:16:46.420] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002394)  
privilege. But when

**[02:16:49.100] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002395)

**[02:16:51.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002396)

**[02:16:53.200] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002397)

But maybe

**[02:22:06.980] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002550)

**[02:22:08.720] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002551)

**[02:22:11.420] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002552)

**[02:22:18.160] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002555)  
So, but we have already explained

**[02:22:20.040] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002556)  
earlier that one of

**[02:22:22.200] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002557)

**[02:22:24.380] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002558)

**[02:22:26.220] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002559)

**[02:22:28.200] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002560)

**[02:22:29.280] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002561)

**[02:22:32.080] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002562)

**[02:22:34.200] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002563)

**[02:22:38.120] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002565)

**[02:22:41.560] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002566)

**[02:22:44.060] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002567)

**[02:22:46.400] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002568)

**[02:22:48.080] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002569)

**[02:22:49.920] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002570)  
The weaknesses that

**[02:22:52.260] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002571)

**[02:22:53.800] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002572)

**[02:22:54.880] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002573)

**[02:22:56.780] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002574)

**[02:22:58.660] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002575)

**[02:23:00.560] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002576)

**[02:23:02.740] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002577)

**[02:23:04.380] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-002578)

**[03:20:04.480] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004235)

**[03:20:33.640] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004250)

**[03:20:48.580] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004257)

**[03:20:58.560] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004262)

**[03:28:10.360] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004477)

**[03:28:21.980] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004482)

**[03:28:23.940] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004483)

**[03:28:40.640] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004491)

**[03:28:44.060] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004493)

**[03:28:46.240] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004494)

**[03:28:48.020] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004495)

**[03:28:56.100] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004499)

**[03:28:57.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004500)

**[03:28:59.840] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004501)

**[03:29:04.060] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004503)  
sa mga pinakamahirap,

**[03:29:06.540] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004504)

**[03:29:08.560] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004505)

**[03:29:11.260] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004506)  
Yan po yung

**[03:29:11.980] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004507)  
one and two

**[03:29:14.420] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004508)

**[03:29:15.840] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004509)  
DBCC. Pangalawa po,

**[03:29:18.600] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004510)

ang

**[03:29:25.860] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004514)  
ay ang mawawala

**[03:29:27.700] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004515)

**[03:29:31.020] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004517)

**[03:29:37.540] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004518)  
Yeah, it's approximately

**[03:29:39.500] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004519)  
12 billion a month kung matanggal.

Ang posisyon ko naman po as

**[03:29:43.580] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004521)

**[03:29:45.800] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004522)

**[03:29:48.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004523)

**[03:29:49.680] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004524)

**[03:29:51.220] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004525)

**[03:29:53.440] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004526)

**[03:30:00.000] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004530)

**[03:30:21.620] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004531)  
And third po is what I mentioned earlier, yung targeted subsidy to the vulnerable sectors. Thank you.

**[03:30:30.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004532)  
Salamat. Ibig sabihin, mas binabalansin nyo. Anyway, about system loss, kagaya na sana nang ginawa ng BIR sa pagtanggal ng value-added tax VAT sa allowable system loss charge.

**[03:30:46.460] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004533)  
We load the DOF and the BIR on this move dahil na rin po sa hirap.

**[03:30:51.620] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004534)  
At ng buhay ngayon, Mr. Chair, marami po sa ating mga kababayan ang dumadaing dahil sa pagtaas ng kanilang electric bills.

**[03:30:57.940] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004535)  
Patuloy rin po ang ating panawagan, natanggalin na lang po ang system loss.

**[03:31:01.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004536)  
According to reports, system loss accounts for around 5% of consumers' monthly electricity bills.

**[03:31:08.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004537)  
Kung ang isang pamilya po ay nagbabayad ng 4,000 kada buwan sa kuryente, nasa halos 200 po agad ang napupunta sa system loss.

**[03:31:18.720] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004538)  
Buwan-buwan po ito.

**[03:31:20.400] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004539)  
At sa totoo lamang po,

**[03:31:21.620] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004540)

**[03:31:25.720] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004541)  
minsan nga hindi nila alam ano ba itong system loss na kanilang binabayaran pa.

**[03:31:30.920] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004542)  
Kaya kaisa tayo sa panawagan, lalo na ng mga Pilipinong nahirapan po sa dami ng gastusin,

**[03:31:36.740] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004543)  
ang system loss ay hindi dapat sinasalo ng mga customer ng electric distributors.

**[03:31:43.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004544)  
Ang system loss ay hindi dapat customer's loss.

**[03:31:47.360] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004545)  
Kung ang system loss ay bunga ng nakaw na kuryente,

**[03:31:50.880] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004546)  
bakit po ang tapat na nagbabayad ng kanilang bill ang sumasalo ng gastos?

**[03:31:56.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004547)  
Hindi ba dapat ang distribution utility ang may kakayahang pigilan po ang illegal connections?

**[03:32:01.840] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004548)  
At nag-file po tayo ng Senate Bill 2360 na naglalayong matanggal ang value-added tax o VAT sa mga additional charges na ito.

**[03:32:10.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004549)  
Also filed Senate Bill 2383 seeking to prohibit distribution utilities and electric cooperatives

**[03:32:19.960] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004550)  
from passing.

**[03:32:20.880] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004551)  
system loss charges on to electric city.

**[03:32:26.640] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004552)  
Ito po yung Senate Bill 2383 seeking to prohibit distribution utilities and electric cooperatives

**[03:32:32.780] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004553)  
from passing system loss charges on to electricity consumers po.

**[03:32:41.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004554)  
Secretary ko.

**[03:32:42.680] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004555)  
Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[03:32:44.880] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004556)  
Tama po yan.

**[03:32:45.920] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004557)  
Nauna na po ang DOF at ang Bureau of Internal Revenue sa pagbibigay.

**[03:32:50.880] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004558)  
Sa pagtanggal ng VAT on systems loss.

**[03:32:58.140] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004559)  
Napakabilis po kami nag-action dyan nung natanggap namin ng instructions on that matter.

**[03:33:05.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004560)  
However, Your Honor, on systems loss, I think the proper department is the DOE and the ERC.

**[03:33:18.840] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004561)  
Hindi po kami...

**[03:33:20.880] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004562)  
Kasali dyan sa discussion on systems loss whatsoever except when it comes to VAT, which

**[03:33:27.000] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004563)

**[03:33:34.140] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-004564)  
systems loss just last month.

**[03:33:38.300] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004565)  
Mr. Chair, on another topic lang po.

**[03:33:40.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004566)  
May kli lang po ito.

**[03:33:42.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004567)  
Alam nun naman po natin ang micro, small, and medium enterprises or MSMEs.

**[03:33:48.300] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004568)  
Ito po yung maliliit na negosyo ay mahalagang bahagi po ng ating ekonomiya.

**[03:33:54.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004569)  
Alam nyo po yun, lalong-lalo na po si Secretary Igo na mula sa business sector po.

**[03:33:59.400] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004570)  
Bago ka pumasok sa gobyerno, may ka pong natulungan ng mga maliliit na negosyante noon.

**[03:34:07.760] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004571)  
Ito pong MSMEs, they provide jobs, livelihood, and opportunities to many of our kababayan.

**[03:34:13.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004572)  
Ngunit sa kabila ng kanilang mahalagang papel,

**[03:34:16.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004573)  
sila pong madalas na nakakarana.

**[03:34:18.300] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004574)  
Sila pong madalas po ng mabigat na requirements at bayarin mula sa iba't-ibang ahensya na gobyerno.

**[03:34:23.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004575)  
At nabanggit nyo po kanina na yun po yung ginagawa ninyo,

**[03:34:26.160] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004576)  
streamlining processes, reduced fees.

**[03:34:30.540] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004577)  
At kung maalala nyo po, during the time of former President Duterte,

**[03:34:34.360] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004578)  
he signed into law Republic Act 11032.

**[03:34:38.160] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004579)  
Ito po yung Ease of Doing Business Act to Reduce Red Tape in Government Services.

**[03:34:43.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004580)  
Sana po yung mapagaan din natin ang mga obligasyon at pasanin ng ating mga MSMEs

**[03:34:48.300] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004581)  
Ito po yung mga maliliit na negosyante.

**[03:34:50.780] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004582)  
Malaking tulong po ito sa kanila.

**[03:34:52.700] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004583)  
Dapat po yung maturuan sila ng tamang proseso para mainganyo ang ating mga kababayan na magnegosyo.

**[03:34:58.200] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004584)  
To the DOF and BIR, what are your efforts in simplifying procedures and tax obligations of MSMEs?

**[03:35:08.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004585)  
Please, BIR, Commissioner Mendoza, please answer the query.

**[03:35:13.360] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004586)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair, Senator Goh.

**[03:35:18.300] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004587)  
Nauunawaan po namin na bagamat ang principal na mandate po ng Bureau of Internal Revenue

**[03:35:23.640] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004588)  
is manglekta ng buwis upang matustusan natin ang mga pangangailangan ng gobyerno.

**[03:35:28.940] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004589)  
But at the same time, we have the equally important mandate of helping in making doing business in the Philippines much easier.

**[03:35:38.140] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004590)  
Kaya yan rin po ay isa sa mga prioridad po ng kasalukuyang administrasyon.

**[03:35:43.100] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004591)  
Patungkol po sa mga micro and small entrepreneurs natin,

**[03:35:46.760] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004592)  
ito po ay nabangkit nga ng Pangulo noong kanyang SONA na sa kasalukuyan po ay nagpapatupad tayo ng isang one-time tax abatement program for our microtax payers.

**[03:35:57.280] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004593)  
Ang mga microtax payers po ay ito po yung ang gross sales sa isang taon ay hindi lumalampas sa 3 million pesos.

**[03:36:04.540] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004594)  
At karaniwan po ay sila ang maraming halimbawa open cases sa BIR, hindi sila nakakapag-file ng returns,

**[03:36:11.560] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004595)  
nagsara na sila, subalit hindi nila alam na kinakailangan din nilang formally na magsara sa BIR.

**[03:36:16.760] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004596)  
Kung kaya't pag bumalik po sila at magtatayo sila ng bagong nebosyo, magpaparehistro, lalabas yung pangalan ng previous business nila at nagkakaproblema po sila sa pagkuhan ng clearance.

**[03:36:27.080] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004597)  
So yan po ay nauunawaan natin. Kaya una po, nagsagawa po tayo ng tinatawag na ease of closing business na pinatipo din po natin ngayong taon.

**[03:36:35.520] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004598)  
Sabi natin kung merong ease of doing business, meron din dapat ease of closing business.

**[03:36:40.240] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004599)  
Sapagkat dati-dati po magsasara ka na ng negosyo, maaring nalugi ka pero mahihirapan ka pang magsara.

**[03:36:46.760] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004600)  
Sapagkat napakaraming requirements na hinihingi at yan po ay nilimitahan na lamang po natin ang requirements.

**[03:36:52.880] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004601)  
At ang isa pong malaking tulong na ginawa natin para sa ating mga maliliit na negosyo, yung ating mga micro-entrepreneurs.

**[03:36:59.200] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004602)  
Dati-dati po kung magsasara ang negosyo, meron po kasi tayong tinatawag na mandatory audit before natin sila bigyan ng clearance to check.

**[03:37:06.060] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004603)  
Na wala po talaga silang paglabag sa ating mga tax regulations.

**[03:37:09.680] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004604)  
But what we did po is tinanggal po natin ang ating mga micro-tax payers from the requirement of a mandatory audit.

**[03:37:16.760] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004605)  
Kaya sa loob lamang po ng tatlong araw, kung kumpleto na ang kanilang dokumento, maaari na po nilang maisara formally ang kanilang registration with BIR.

**[03:37:26.760] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004606)  
Ngayon po, na-realize namin na bagamat sinimplihan natin ang mga requirements,

**[03:37:32.600] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004607)  
napakarami po mga maliliit na negosyo, micro-small entrepreneurs.

**[03:37:36.280] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004608)  
Micro-entrepreneurs in particular na marami na po silang tax liability sa BIR na nanahihirapan silang bayaran.

**[03:37:42.420] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004609)  
Kung kaya po naglunsag po tayo ngayong taon ng tinatawag natin na tax abatement.

**[03:37:46.760] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004610)  
Program for micro-tax payers.

**[03:37:49.000] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004611)  
At ikinalulugod ko pong ipaalam sa inyo na sa loob lamang po ng dalawang buwan simula nang inulunsad natin ito,

**[03:37:56.980] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004612)  
ay nakareceive na po kami ng 6,300 more or less na applications for tax abatement.

**[03:38:02.820] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004613)  
At mahigit 5,000 na po ang na-approve natin.

**[03:38:05.820] BIR Commissioner Charlito Martin R. Mendoza** (asr-004614)  
At ito po ay nangangahulugan ng 80 plus million in tax liabilities na na-condone po natin sa pagbabayad lamang nila ng 5,000 pesos na tax abatement.

**[03:39:46.520] SEC Chairperson Francisco Ed Lim** (asr-004640)

that's not only

**[03:42:27.780] SEC Chairperson Francisco Ed Lim** (asr-004679)

**[04:00:17.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005589)

**[04:00:18.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005590)

**[04:00:21.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005591)

**[04:00:22.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005592)

**[04:00:24.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005593)  
Ang hirap na nga tayo sa foreign investments

**[04:00:26.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005594)  
and the reason why I brought this up

**[04:00:28.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005595)

**[04:00:30.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005596)

**[04:00:33.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005597)

**[04:00:37.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005598)  
abuse.

**[04:00:40.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005599)  
So I'm hoping that you'll pay attention.

**[04:00:42.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005600)  
Hirap na nga tayo kung muna investments.

**[04:00:44.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005601)

**[04:00:47.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005602)

**[04:00:50.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005603)

**[04:00:52.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005604)

**[04:00:55.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005605)

**[04:00:58.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005606)

**[04:01:00.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005607)

**[04:01:49.040] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-005630)

**[04:01:50.880] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-005631)  
Systems generated na po ito

**[04:01:52.580] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-005632)

**[04:01:54.520] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-005633)

**[04:01:57.440] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-005634)  
Pinipili na po ng

**[04:01:58.440] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-005635)

**[04:02:00.040] BOC Commissioner Ariel F. Nepomuceno** (asr-005636)
